# Agents · F · part 10

965 entries, fixme-execute-plan to flow-tester. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fixme-execute-plan**: Executes implementation plans step by step with maximum reliability. Follows plan exactly, verifies after every task, enforces final verification gate. Never improvises beyond the plan. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-execute-plan.md))
- **fixme-handle-code-review**: Triages code review findings using the unified taxonomy plus edge-case validity classifications (FIX_FAIL_FAST, ASK_USER_VALIDITY, REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE, REJECT_UNSUPPORTED). Reads plan, spec, and implementation to distinguish real issues from false positives. Outputs HANDLER_RESULT routing directive. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-handle-code-review.md))
- **fixme-handle-plan-review**: Triages plan review findings using the unified taxonomy plus edge-case validity classifications (FIX_FAIL_FAST, ASK_USER_VALIDITY, REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE, REJECT_UNSUPPORTED). Reads codebase to verify each finding. Outputs HANDLER_RESULT routing directive. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-handle-plan-review.md))
- **fixme-handle-spec-review**: Triages specification review findings using the unified taxonomy plus edge-case validity classifications (FIX_FAIL_FAST, ASK_USER_VALIDITY, REJECT_IMPOSSIBLE, REJECT_UNSUPPORTED). Reads the specification and directly referenced context to verify each finding. Outputs HANDLER_RESULT routing directive. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-handle-spec-review.md))
- **fixme-investigate**: Reproduces bugs in a real browser and investigates codebase to find root cause. Writes investigation reports. Does not fix bugs or propose fix approaches. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-investigate.md))
- **fixme-research**: Explores codebase around a known issue to find relevant files, trace references, assess impact, and identify approach candidates. Does not fix bugs or write code. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-research.md))
- **fixme-review-code**: Reviews code produced by plan execution. Finds real bugs, gaps, test issues, and inconsistencies. Read-only - produces structured findings grounded in evidence. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-review-code.md))
- **fixme-review-plan**: Reviews implementation plans for correctness, completeness, and feasibility. Read-only - produces structured findings with evidence and severity. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-review-plan.md))
- **fixme-review-spec**: Reviews specifications for deterministic implementability. Read-only - produces evidence-backed findings about ambiguity, conflicts, gaps, and untestable acceptance criteria. Never modifies any files. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-review-spec.md))
- **fixme-task**: Config-driven pipeline orchestrator. Dispatches sub-skills as agents, manages review loops, compact review context packets, task code map paths, artifact handoff, decision persistence, and ticket state transitions. Never reads source code or edits files directly. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-task.md))
- **fixme-write-plan**: Writes implementation plans and task-scoped code maps by reading the codebase thoroughly. Produces only the plan document and matching code map - never modifies source code. Supports fresh, plan revision, code revision, and rewrite modes. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-write-plan.md))
- **fixme-write-product-spec**: Writes product specifications by reading product context and current user-visible behavior. Produces only product specification documents - never modifies source code. Supports fresh, specification revision, and rewrite modes. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-write-product-spec.md))
- **fixme-write-technical-spec**: Writes technical specifications by reading source material and the codebase thoroughly. Produces only technical specification documents - never modifies source code. Supports fresh, specification revision, and rewrite modes. ([denis-pingin/fixme](https://github.com/denis-pingin/fixme/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixme-write-technical-spec.md))
- **fixp-critic**: A synthesis (`fixC.md`) was written based on YOUR plan alongside another independent plan that you have not seen. C committed to a position on the Surgical-Robust spectrum and produced an isolated review of YOUR work. ([SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit](https://github.com/SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit/blob/HEAD/skills/adversarial-fix-plan/agents/fixp-critic.md))
- **fixp-reconciler**: You synthesized `fixC.md` from two independent fix plans. Both original planners have now critiqued your synthesis from their Round 1 contexts. Your job is to reconcile their feedback against the diagnosis and the code, and produce the final `fix-plan.md`. ([SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit](https://github.com/SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit/blob/HEAD/skills/adversarial-fix-plan/agents/fixp-reconciler.md))
- **fixp-reviser**: You are revising an existing `fix-plan.md` (v1) based on feedback in `fix-review.md` produced by the fix-plan-reviewer stage. The deliberation is narrow: incorporate review feedback into v2. ([SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit](https://github.com/SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit/blob/HEAD/skills/adversarial-fix-plan/agents/fixp-reviser.md))
- **fixp-synthesizer**: You are the **Synthesizer** — an impartial technical reviewer who reads two independent fix plans for the same diagnosed bug and produces a synthesis that lands at the right point on the Surgical-Robust spectrum, justified by the diagnosis confidence, deployment environment, and blast radius. ([SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit](https://github.com/SirTheOracle/forge-toolkit/blob/HEAD/skills/adversarial-fix-plan/agents/fixp-synthesizer.md))
- **FixSecurityAgent.agent**: Use when: fixing security vulnerabilities, OWASP issues, injection flaws, authentication/authorization bugs, or any defect where the current code violates a security requirement. The FixSecurityAgent identifies the root cause of each security defect, applies a minimal targeted fix, and verifies with typecheck + lint + tests. ([kishimin-ai-create/diary](https://github.com/kishimin-ai-create/diary/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/FixSecurityAgent.agent.md))
- **FixTestAgent.agent**: Use when: fixing failing tests, broken assertions, test crashes, or timeouts. The FixTestAgent determines whether the implementation or the test itself is wrong, applies the correct fix, and verifies the full suite passes. ([kishimin-ai-create/diary](https://github.com/kishimin-ai-create/diary/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/FixTestAgent.agent.md))

- **fk-reviewer**: Reviews a candidate diff before it is evaluated - API preservation, hidden CPU syncs, static-shape assumptions, dtype policy, simplicity, duplicate ideas already tried. - **fk-verifier**: Debugs correctness gate failures (numerical mismatch, non-determinism, edge cases, NaN) of a fast-kernel candidate against the frozen reference, and proposes the minimal fix. - **fkit-adversarial-reviewer**: Adversarial second-opinion reviewer. Ask it to attack a diff/scope or a plan (with an optional focus area) for an independent hostile pass that hunts bugs, edge cases, security holes, races, regressions, and missing tests. Runs the review on Codex (via the codex CLI) for genuine model diversity; degrades to a Claude pass with a loud flag when codex is unavailable. Returns FINDINGS ONLY — never edits code, never commits. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-adversarial-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-architect**: Software architect. Invoke for a focused design-consistency / feasibility / cost / risk consult, or to run "survey-project" (evidence-first codebase survey that writes ai-agents/knowledge-base/architecture.md — used during project initiation). Designs, never implements (interface stubs only); never writes the wiki; never commits. Can consult the producer for the product context behind a technical decision. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-architect.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-coder**: Implementation agent — the sole source-write authority. Takes a task from a brief to working, tested code: plan first, get approval, make the minimal correct change, prove it works. Never commits unprompted. NOT for background delegation — implementation needs the owner present for its plan and fix approval gates, so it runs as a session (`fkit coder`). Can consult the architect (design consistency) and producer (scope). ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-coder.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-lead**: The fkit lead and orchestrating conductor — the first entry in the `fkit` menu. Two capabilities in one agent: it ROUTES ("who should I talk to about this?", reads the wiki, one-off @role consults), and it DRIVES — given a goal it spawns whatever typed fkit-&lt;role&gt; subagents it needs, gives each a bounded unit of work, relays any surfaced decision to the owner, and advances until the work is done. Its flagship driver is /fkit-sprint-ship-loop (ships a whole sprint). It delegates the real work to fresh role contexts; it never writes source, reviews, or diagnoses itself. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-lead.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-producer**: Product / sprint-planning agent. Invoke for a focused product question (priority, scope, user need, timeline) or a sprint/backlog status summary. Plans sprints, writes task briefs, tracks status. Never writes code. Owns the task AND sprint lifecycle via /fkit-task-done, /fkit-task-cancelled, /fkit-sprint-done and /fkit-sprint-cancelled — the ONLY role that may invoke any of the four, so every other role routes its closes here. Can consult the architect for the technical picture behind a product call. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-producer.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-reviewer**: Independent code reviewer. Ask for a review with a scope (working tree, or a branch vs a base ref) and a mode — ephemeral (report only) or stateful (findings recorded into the shared review.md ledger inside the task folder) — or, as phase 2 of a stateful review, with the owner's residual dispositions to record. Runs two passes: its own, plus a Codex adversarial second opinion via the codex CLI. REVIEW-ONLY — writes only review ledgers (a task folder's review.md, or a sprint-keyed ledger under ai-agents/sprints/reviews/), never source code, never commits. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **fkit-wiki**: The project wiki librarian — the EXCLUSIVE write gateway for ai-agents/wiki-vault/. Ask it to run an ingest (add/update pages from a source), a lint (health-check the vault), a sync (delta-ingest what changed since the last sync), or a deep multi-step wiki research question. Simple lookups don't need it — any session can run the read-only /fkit-query procedure. Reads and writes wiki files; never commits. ([flashist/fkit](https://github.com/flashist/fkit/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/fkit-wiki.md) · ★1)

- **fleet-reviewer.agent**: ../../.claude/agents/fleet-reviewer.md ([sturlabragason/fleet](https://github.com/sturlabragason/fleet/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/fleet-reviewer.agent.md) · ★3)
- **fleet-round**: Run the machine half of ONE fleet round — read the router's step files yourself, run the sweep, the drive leg, the keep-alive leg, the own-parks leg, the open leg and the manager reap, persist the snapshot, and return the ≤ 1 KB verdict hand-back plus the payload path. Dispatched by router row 1 of /supervisor:fleet-drive. The manager keeps only the re-arm, the operator-facing rows and the render. ([bborbe/claude-supervisor](https://github.com/bborbe/claude-supervisor/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-round.md))
- **fleet-rule-auditor**: Keeps the fleet's rules, role files, contracts and fleet definitions from turning into bureaucracy. Weighs every rule line by what it costs the seats against what it has prevented, and cuts, merges or shortens lines; it never adds one. Runs after every fleet run closes (mike starts it from the close step and closes the session once its turn is done), when the fleet text is over its budget, and on a draft definition before ready. ([temet-ai/mike](https://github.com/temet-ai/mike/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-rule-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **fleet-rules-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for any Lab Manager fleet diff or PR. Use before pushing to dev or before a promotion to check the change against lm/.github/fleet-rules.md (tenant scope, vault secrets, UUIDs in the UI, blocking async, twins, docs, comments) and to run the same checks CI runs. ([lbockenstedt/ab](https://github.com/lbockenstedt/ab/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/fleet-rules-reviewer.agent.md) · 4 copies)
- **FLEET-RUNTIME**: What every transient fleet role (worker, merger, both reviewers, smoke-worker, epic-steward, design-answerer) does at startup and exit; role files point here. The architect (`role-opus-architect.md`) is interactive and skips the loop ceremonies (heartbeat, reservation check, per-iteration shutdown) but shares the cache read and the feedback file. ([jakildev/IrredenEngine](https://github.com/jakildev/IrredenEngine/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/FLEET-RUNTIME.md) · ★10)
- **fleet-safety-reviewer**: Adversarial review of changes that reach the Nexus Qs in the field — through a flashed image or an OTA `apk upgrade` — against the repo's non-negotiables — no bricking write, every fix baked into its package with a pkgrel bump, OTA and A/B paths that cannot strand a unit, a unit keeps its identity, no personal access in public artifacts, stock parity for kernel/DTS work, device limits (2 cores, a 25 W amp). Use proactively before committing changes under pmos/, userspace/, kernel/, scripts/ or docker-build.sh. Read-only; returns ranked findings. ([petronijus/nexusQ-reloaded](https://github.com/petronijus/nexusQ-reloaded/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-safety-reviewer.md) · ★13)
- **fleet-scenario**: Walks a whole browser scenario in the parent session's pane and returns findings as bounded JSON, holding the screenshots and DOM reads in its own context. Use for a multi step UI walk. The caller sets the model from the brief. One spawn per scenario: a single probe is cheaper run inline. ([makarasty/makarasty-claude](https://github.com/makarasty/makarasty-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-scenario.md))
- **fleet-scheduler**: Deterministic fleet scheduling and ticket DAG orchestration agent for Antigravity Booster. ([voodootikigod/antigravity-booster](https://github.com/voodootikigod/antigravity-booster/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-scheduler.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-security**: Security audit agent. Checks for OWASP top 10 vulnerabilities, missing authentication, injection vectors, hardcoded secrets, and missing access control. Spawned on demand or as part of fleet-review. ([g6xai/claude-plugins](https://github.com/g6xai/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet/agents/fleet-security.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-security**: Ingeniero Senior de Seguridad de Aplicaciones especializado en APIs, autenticación, autorización, RBAC, multi-tenant, protección de datos personales, tracking público y seguridad para plataformas logísticas. ([cplazaibarra/plataforma-transporte](https://github.com/cplazaibarra/plataforma-transporte/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/security.md))
- **fleet-security-auditor**: Fleet-specific security analysis covering MDM, osquery, API auth, and device management threat models. Use when touching auth, MDM, enrollment, or user data. ([fleetdm/fleet](https://github.com/fleetdm/fleet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-security-auditor.md) · ★7.2k · 2 copies)
- **fleet-slack-check**: Reads one Slack review-request source for the fleet manager and returns only candidate PR lines. Read-only. Used by the fleet-manager skill. ([klaidliadon/claude-plugins](https://github.com/klaidliadon/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet/agents/fleet-slack-check.md))
- **fleet-status**: Check the full status of the media server fleet - all services, libraries, downloads, and disk usage. Use when the user asks about their media server status or health. ([maxtechera/admirarr](https://github.com/maxtechera/admirarr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-status.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-steward**: Keeps the fleet's definitions current. Runs weekly and unattended, watching for model and tooling changes, running the migration checklist over every agent body, running the evals, and auditing installed plugins. It files and proposes, and never merges. ([rzem-ai/coder-fleet](https://github.com/rzem-ai/coder-fleet/blob/HEAD/claude/coder-fleet/agents/fleet-steward.md))
- **fleet-strategist**: Fleet-level pattern analyst focused on cross-repo systemic risks, portfolio balance, and kit-level improvements. Used by cross-repo-insight. ([chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology](https://github.com/chrismmorin-ux/Perspective-Cosmology/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-strategist.md))
- **fleet-supervisor**: Read-only triage of a third-party fleet's open PR set (e.g. the Cursor automation fleet) BEFORE the owner merges. Runs the deterministic util/fleet_triage/predict_merge.py predicted-merge per PR, then adjudicates dup/supersession, builds the same-file cluster map, computes a stale-branch-minimizing merge order, and returns per-PR verdicts + an ordered plan as its final message. Use when the deliverable is a merge-batch plan over many in-flight fleet PRs. Read-only (Read, Grep, Glob, Bash); never pushes, merges, closes, or authors a PR. ([pcalnon/juniper-ml](https://github.com/pcalnon/juniper-ml/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fleet-supervisor.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-sweep-reader**: Compute the fleet sweep's read half (Steps 0b–3 of /supervisor:fleet-loop) — read the four channels and the open-items ledger, diff against the previous snapshot, run the orphan reverse index, find collision and unmanaged-topic candidates, classify every session, persist the next snapshot through fleet-snapshot.py, write the digest render to its payload path, and return the ≤ 1 KB bucket-count hand-back. Never acts, never messages, never writes anything but the snapshot and the payload. ([bborbe/claude-supervisor](https://github.com/bborbe/claude-supervisor/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-sweep-reader.md))
- **fleet-sync-agent**: Synchronization agent. Diffs repository spec state against Linear and Notion, reconciles differences. Repo always wins. Can be scheduled to run on a cron for continuous sync. ([g6xai/claude-plugins](https://github.com/g6xai/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/fleet/agents/fleet-sync-agent.md) · ★1)
- **fleet-sync-auditor**: Four-tier propagation audit after a release or an identity change, per the sync-reference-docs skill: did the change reach the repo's own docs (VERSION carriers, patchnotes placement, roadmap ticks, README badge URLs, AGENTS.md status headers, Flatpak runtime pins), the workspace catalog row (version, phase, prose, stack cell, verified stamp), the public surfaces (the profile README's shipping tags, the site codex status spans), and the Obsidian vault's identity stubs (identity only; the vault never carries versions)? Dispatch it after a release lands, after a rename, retirement, absorption,… ([VirInvictus/dragon-agents](https://github.com/VirInvictus/dragon-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/fleet-sync-auditor.md) · ★5)

- **fleet-verifier**: Re-runs one stated command, or checks one stated fact, and reports the literal result with its exit code and the size of the set it looked at. - **fleet-worker**: fleet 的执行会话——一个任务、一个 worktree、一个 PR：读任务记录 T&lt;n&gt;、实现、开 PR、门禁全绿后自己合并、收口 T 记录、向派它的会话回报（不认领、不评论、不关闭、不建普通 GitHub issue；唯一例外：会话仅可在 GuangZhouShanyouGame/24haowan-monorepo 通过仓库自带的 `node tools/cd/fragments-migrate.js --new &lt;file.md&gt;` 创建带 `release-fragment` 标签的 issue，随后按 `doc/release-ledger/fragment-schema.md` 更新这份 issue 的正文；认领、评论任务进度、关闭 issue、创建普通 issue 仍禁止，其他仓库不适用。第一句是人的原话，流程由 SessionStart hook 引入）。 ([verkyyi/claude-fleet](https://github.com/verkyyi/claude-fleet/blob/HEAD/conf/agent-defaults/claude/agents/fleet-worker.md) · ★2)

- **fleeting-researcher**: Researcher with two independent modes: (1) charter scoring, which rates unscored fleeting notes against one domain charter for focused/bootstrap curation, appending scores to a per-domain ratings cache; (2) landscape mapping, which reads every domain charter and, for each fleeting note, lists every bundle it overlaps, appending to a shared landscape cache. Never curates, never writes ledgers, fleeting notes, or wiki bundles in either mode.
